## 1. Objective & Scope

### Objective
Design and implement a **repository-agnostic, GitHub-native, self-hostable centralized AI SDLC orchestrator service** in `johnhain-msft/mfg_msft_ai_sdlc` (`C:\Users\johnhain\Documents\mfg_msft_ai_sdlc`) built on a GitHub App [F435, F442, F443, F495, F501], GitHub Actions [F497, F511, F520], and `gh-aw` (GitHub Agentic Workflows) [F343, F347, F552, F553] with zero external server infrastructure. Users can point the service at **any** upstream or downstream GitHub repository with declarative instructions and dependency edges, and the pipeline dynamically configures, reconciles, and executes itself. The service delivers five capabilities:
1. **Generic Declarative Fleet Manifest (`sdlc.yaml`) & Auto-Onboarding Agent (`sdlc onboard`)**: Accept a single declarative `sdlc.yaml` manifest defining arbitrary `projects[]` (any `<owner>/<repo>`) and directed `dependencyEdges[]` (`upstreamProject -> downstreamProject`, supporting multi-repo fan-in, fan-out, and multi-hop DAGs) with per-project instructions, upstream drift-watch sources, preflight/build/verification gate commands, immutable gate policies, and PR reviewer/branch policies; AND provide an automated onboarding CLI/agent (`node bin/sdlc-onboard.mjs <owner/repo> [--downstream <owner/repo>] [--instructions "..."]` and `.github/workflows/sdlc-onboard.md`) that inspects any target repository's `package.json`, `.github/workflows/`, test scripts, and provenance files to auto-generate its validated `sdlc.yaml` configuration block and reconcile the pipeline. Ship the live portfolio fleet (`johnhain-msft/gh-admin-msft` [F30, F56, F307], `ms-mfg-community/day-in-the-life-copilot-lab` [F28, F35, F142, F166, F172], `ms-mfg-community/github-pipeline-showcase` [F91, F115], and `githubschool/gh-github-intermediate-template` [F183, F184] cascading into `johnhain-msft/portfolio` [F1, F20]) purely as declarative `sdlc.yaml` data rather than hardcoded engine logic.
2. **Self-Reconciling Pipeline (`src/reconcile.mjs`)**: Automatically reconcile and compile parameterized central orchestration workflows (`.github/workflows/drift-<projectKey>.md`, `.github/workflows/cascade-<edgeId>.md`, `.github/workflows/cascade-router.yml`, and `.lock.yml` via `gh aw compile --strict --purge` [F347, F348, F353, F354, F369, F373]) whenever `sdlc.yaml` changes (whether edited by hand or via `sdlc onboard`), eliminating manual per-repository automation setup [F552] and supporting opt-in schedule deconfliction plus open-PR deduplication for pre-existing in-repo scheduled automations [F191, F198, F217, F222, F225, F234, F554].
3. **Generic Upstream Drift, Library Modernization & Code-Improvement Automation (`S2`)**: For any `projects[]` entry with `driftAudit.enabled == true`, execute the user's registration-time `instructions` (inline or `instructions/<projectKey>.md`), `when` schedule/triggers, and `upkeepStrategy` (`enum("content-and-docs-sync", "dependency-and-pattern-modernization", "code-quality-and-improvement-scan", "custom")`) against the project's declared `knowledgeSources` — including arbitrary user-specified `watchUrls` (changelogs, blogs, release notes, and documentation websites snapshotted in `pre-agent-steps:` into `/tmp/gh-aw/agent/` [F364, F365] and allowlisted by hostname in the compiled `gh-aw` `network` frontmatter [F350, F354]), `packageManifests` (`package.json`, `*.csproj`, etc. scanned for new library/framework versions and pattern migrations such as Angular `17 -> 18` via `context7` MCP), and `mcpServers` (`microsoft-learn`, `context7`) [F194, F197, F203-F208, F361] — then execute target-repository `buildCommands` and `verifyCommands` in `post-steps:` [F364, F365] and open or update automated pull requests (or draft suggestion PRs) on the target repository [F198, F225, F388, F393-F395, F407, F414, F416, F435, F494]. In the shipped `sdlc.yaml` fleet preset, this drives GHEC documentation/changelog audits for `johnhain-msft/gh-admin-msft` [F280-F342] and `docs/_meta/registry.yaml` + `self-improving-agents.md` audits for `ms-mfg-community/day-in-the-life-copilot-lab` [F191-F279, F375].
4. **Dynamic Downstream Dependency Cascade (`S3` & `S4` — Triggered by Automated PR Merges OR Direct Manual Commits)**: Detect **any** merged PR, direct commit push to an upstream `defaultBranch`, or scheduled refresh across all `dependencyEdges[]` in `sdlc.yaml` via `S3`'s data-driven router (`cascade-router.yml`, sole owner and writer of per-edge Actions variables `SDLC_SYNC_STATE_<SLUG>` [F524-F529, F586, F592, F600, F610] serialized under `concurrency: { group: "sdlc-state-<edgeId>", "cancel-in-progress": false }` [F350]) and cascade changes into the declared `downstreamProject` whether the upstream change came from an `S2` PR or a human maintainer's direct update:
   - **Generic Deterministic Mechanical Cascade Engine (`S3`, `mode: "mechanical"`)**: Executes the edge's declared `preflightCommands` in `cascade-router.yml` to short-circuit commit-only or timestamp-only no-ops (`lastNoopHeadSha`) before dispatching `gh-aw`, runs the edge's declared `importCommands` during the `agent` phase, commits the updated `allowedFiles` + `provenance.file` into the git bundle prior to `create_pull_request` [F392, `OQ37`], and runs the downstream project's `buildCommands` and `verifyCommands` in `post-steps:`. In the shipped `sdlc.yaml` preset, this configures `gh-admin-msft -> portfolio` (`npm run import:ghec-admin -- <checkout>` writing `public/ghec-admin/**` and `public/ghec-admin/source.json` [F13, F30, F39-F87]).
   - **Generic Bounded AI-Authored Adaptation Engine (`S4`, `mode: "ai-adaptation"`)**: Executes the edge's declared read-only `preflightCommands` in `pre-agent-steps:` (capturing drift reports under `/tmp/gh-aw/agent/` [F365]), runs generic configurable revision-pin updates (`node "$GITHUB_WORKSPACE/src/bump-revision-pins.mjs" --config <edge-json> --new-sha "$UPSTREAM_SHA"`), guides the `gh-aw` agent using the edge's `adaptationInstructions` to adapt files within `allowedFiles` across cumulative `lastMergedUpstreamSha..upstream_sha`, runs the edge's `acceptCommands` and `buildCommands` and commits all outputs before calling `create_pull_request` [F392, `OQ37`], and gates every PR in `post-steps:` with the generic policy-driven `node --frozen-intrinsics "$GITHUB_WORKSPACE/src/verify-immutable-gates.mjs" --portfolio <downstream-dir> --base "$BASE_SHA" --edge <edgeId>` (which enforces the edge's `immutableGates.zeroDiffPaths`, AST-locked `lockedTestBlocks`, `inProcessInvariants` with `Object.freeze`, and Vitest `--reporter=json` pass checks) plus the downstream project's `verifyCommands`. In the shipped `sdlc.yaml` preset, this configures: (i) `day-in-the-life-copilot-lab -> portfolio` (`sync:workshop-source` [F13, F28, F34, F128-F166], `experiences/copilot-workshop/content/*.ts` excluding `rules.ts`/`source.ts`/`types.ts`, `public/LICENSE`, `public/NOTICE.txt`, `README.md`, `public/copilot-project.html`, `tests/copilot-workshop.test.ts` under `content/rules.ts` 140-WPM/word-cap/227-min invariants [F24-F27, F147-F150], and `src/hash-manifest-drift.mjs` / `apps/lab-studio` `lab01`–`lab20` sync [F3, F11, F35, F142, F169-F182, F190, `OQ21`]); (ii) `github-pipeline-showcase -> portfolio` (`experiences/pipeline-showcase/content/provenance.json`, `fetch-run-data.mjs` `RUNS` IDs/stories, `tests/pipeline-showcase.test.ts`, and `npm run build:experiences` [F7, F9, F33, F88-F114]); and (iii) `gh-github-intermediate-template -> portfolio` (`experiences/github-intermediate-labs/provenance.json`, `experiences/github-intermediate-{labs,workshop}/**`, `tests/github-intermediate-{labs,workshop}.test.ts`, and `npm run build:experiences` [F9, F183-F185]); all gated by the 5 portfolio verification commands (`typecheck`, `lint`, `test`, `test:studio`, `test:browser` [F10, F11, F15, F16]).
5. **Turnkey Packaging, One-Command Bootstrap & Auto-Onboarding CLI (`S5`)**: Package `mfg_msft_ai_sdlc` as a self-contained template/forkable repository and `gh-aw` package (`aw.yml` [F380, F613]) with: (a) `node bin/sdlc-bootstrap.mjs` to register the GitHub App via the 3-step manifest handshake [F556-F566, F603-F609], guide installation across user/organization accounts [F461-F468, F571-F575], provision repository/organization Actions variables (creating `SDLC_SYNC_STATE_<SLUG>` only when missing on `404`) and libsodium-encrypted secrets [F443, F581-F600, F607-F612], optionally disable duplicate in-repo schedules on operator-owned upstreams (`deconflictInRepoSchedules: "disable-workflow-schedules"`, default `"none"`) [F191, F222, F554], and invoke `S1` to compile the workflows; and (b) `node bin/sdlc-onboard.mjs` (plus `.github/workflows/sdlc-onboard.md`) so any user can point the orchestrator at a new repository and instructions to automatically generate its `sdlc.yaml` project/edge entries and open a self-reconciling PR on the orchestrator repo.

### In Scope
- **Generic Fleet Manifest Schema, Policy Renderer, Reconciler & Onboarding Validator (`S1`)**: Authoring `schemas/sdlc.schema.json`, `sdlc.yaml` (the portfolio reference preset), and `src/reconcile.mjs` to transform any valid `sdlc.yaml` into parameterized `.github/workflows/drift-<projectKey>.md`, `.github/workflows/cascade-<edgeId>.md`, `.github/workflows/cascade-router.yml`, `.github/workflows/sdlc-onboard.md`, `.github/workflows/aw.json` (`strict: true`, `protected_files.exclude` [F353, F374, F405]), and compiled `.lock.yml` files via `gh aw compile --strict --purge` [F347, F353, F354, F367, F369, F373, F374], rendering `on.bots: ["<app-slug>[bot]"]` [F363, F543], per-edge `concurrency` groups [F350], `permissions`, `safe-outputs.threat-detection` [F386], edge-scoped `protected-files` / `allowed-files` / `exclude-lock-yml` [F403, F405], deterministic PR branch identity (`preserve-branch-name: true`, `recreate-ref: true`, `close-older-pull-requests: true`, `close-older-key` [F393-F395, F407]), explicit `reviewers` [F388], and `auto-merge: false` [F236, F407].
- **Data-Driven Upstream Drift Automation (`S2`)**: Parameterized `gh-aw` workflows (`CentralRepoOps` [F552]) generated from `sdlc.yaml` `projects[].driftAudit` entries (configured in the default preset for `johnhain-msft/gh-admin-msft` and `ms-mfg-community/day-in-the-life-copilot-lab`, with open-PR deduplication against existing `automation/*` and `sdlc/*` PRs [F198, F217, F225, F234] and `gh_aw_schema_version` `0.86.2` treated as report-only [F204, F210, F236, F241, F253, F381, F403]), executing project-declared `buildCommands` before `create_pull_request` [F392, `OQ37`], running project-declared `verifyCommands` in `post-steps:` (`DeterministicOps` [F364, F365], including isolated `GH_DATA_DIR` support when a target repo pins an older `gh-aw` version [F241, F253, F375]), and creating verified cross-repository pull requests via `safe-outputs.create-pull-request` [F388, F414, F416].
- **Data-Driven Cross-Repo Event Routing, State Ownership & Mechanical Cascade (`S3`)**: Central conditional-GET (`ETag` / `If-None-Match` [F524-F530]) upstream commit/merged-PR and scheduled refresh router (`cascade-router.yml`) driven by `sdlc.yaml` `dependencyEdges[]`, acting as the **sole owner and writer** of per-edge Actions variables `SDLC_SYNC_STATE_<SLUG>` [F586, F592, F600, F610] serialized per edge under `concurrency: { group: "sdlc-state-<upstreamKey>", "cancel-in-progress": false }` [F350], running edge-declared `preflightCommands` (`sync:workshop-source`, `node "$GITHUB_WORKSPACE/src/lab-studio-drift.mjs"`, `snapshotAt`-normalized `fetch-run-data.mjs`, and `git diff` path filters) to short-circuit commit-only and timestamp-only no-ops (`lastNoopHeadSha`) before dispatching `gh-aw`, executing `mode: "mechanical"` imports (`import:ghec-admin` [F30, F39-F87], restricted by `allowed-files: ["public/ghec-admin/**"]` [F405]) committed prior to `create_pull_request` [F392, `OQ37`], dispatching `S4` asynchronously via `workflow_dispatch` (`X-GitHub-Api-Version: 2022-11-28`, `return_run_details: true` [F474, F517]) with `previous_sha = lastMergedUpstreamSha`, recording `inFlightRunId` / `inFlightTargetSha` in `SDLC_SYNC_STATE_<SLUG>`, and exiting immediately so the queued `S4` run acquires the `sdlc-state-<upstreamKey>` lock, running downstream `verifyCommands` in `post-steps:` [F10, F11, F15, F16], and updating `SDLC_SYNC_STATE_<SLUG>` in post-`safe_outputs` (`if: success()`) and failure-escalation (`if: failure()`) jobs (`needs: [agent, detection, safe_outputs]`, inheriting the workflow-level `concurrency` group without a job-level `concurrency` declaration [F350, F364, F371]) after verifying either that `AGENT_HEAD_SHA == BASE_SHA` (`outcome: "noop-no-changes"`) or that `GET /repos/{downstream}/pulls?state=open&head={owner}:<branchName>` [F393, F473] returns an open PR whose on-branch provenance file at `pr.head.sha` records `upstream_sha` (`fallback-as-issue: false` [F389]).
- **Data-Driven Bounded AI Downstream Adaptation (`S4`)**: Parameterized `gh-aw` adaptation workflows generated from `sdlc.yaml` `dependencyEdges[]` (`mode: "ai-adaptation"`) plus generic, config-driven helpers (`src/lab-studio-drift.mjs` for 16-hex LF-normalised SHA-256 manifest drift, `src/bump-studio-revision-pins.mjs` for declarative 40-hex/7-hex revision-pin replacement, and `src/verify-immutable-gates.mjs` for declarative zero-diff, AST test-block, and frozen-module invariant checks). ### Out of Scope
- External always-on webhook servers, external databases, or non-GitHub compute (all workflows run on GitHub Actions Linux runners `ubuntu-latest` / `ubuntu-24.04` [F343, F356, F553]).
- Autonomous or automated PR merging (`auto-merge` is forbidden on every `safe-outputs.create-pull-request` handler and CLI flow [F236, F379, F407]); every automated PR requires explicit human review and merge (via `.github/CODEOWNERS` in `ms-mfg-community/day-in-the-life-copilot-lab` [F215, F234] and explicit `safe-outputs.create-pull-request.reviewers` [F388] on `johnhain-msft/gh-admin-msft` and `johnhain-msft/portfolio` after all mandatory `post-steps:` verification gates pass).
- Automated regeneration of `/pipeline-showcase/media/pipeline-demo.svg` from live Playwright screenshots of GitHub Actions UI runs (for which `README.md` notes the capture scripts live in `experiences/pipeline-showcase/capture/` without naming a command or npm script [F14, F32, F113, F115]); when `S4` updates `RUNS` IDs in `fetch-run-data.mjs`, the PR body flags `pipeline-demo.svg` for manual visual re-stitching if needed.
- Automatic bumping of `gh_aw_schema_version` (`0.86.2` [F241, F253, F381]) or automated editing/recompiling of `.github/workflows/*.lock.yml` inside `ms-mfg-community/day-in-the-life-copilot-lab` during routine drift sweeps (`S2` reports new `github/gh-aw` releases in `docs/_meta/audit-report.md` [F204, F210, F212] and enforces `exclude-lock-yml: true` [F403] and `protected-files: "blocked"` on `.github/workflows/**` [F236, F405]).
- Modifying Azure Container Apps runtime infrastructure, `Microsoft.App/sandboxGroups` Bicep/Terraform, or live Azure entitlement tests (`npm run test:live -w server`) that require interactive user entitlements or live Azure sandboxes [F17, F21-F23, F36, F182].
- Shipping customer-specific authoring tools from `githubschool/gh-github-intermediate-template` [F183] or rewriting upstream lab prose during scheduled staleness sweeps [F191, F211].

### Assumptions & Foundational Design Decisions
- **Design Decision — Generic Data-Driven Engine vs. Declarative Fleet Preset (`sdlc.yaml`) & Auto-Onboarding (`sdlc onboard`)**: The orchestrator engine (`src/reconcile.mjs`, `cascade-router.yml`, `src/update-poll-state.mjs`, `src/lab-studio-drift.mjs`, `src/bump-studio-revision-pins.mjs`, `src/verify-immutable-gates.mjs`, `bin/sdlc-bootstrap.mjs`, and `bin/sdlc-onboard.mjs`) is **100% repository-agnostic**: no engine schema or script hardcodes a fixed list of repository names. Instead, `schemas/sdlc.schema.json` accepts any `projects[]` entry (`projectKey: /^[a-z0-9][a-z0-9-]{0,62}$/`) and any directed `dependencyEdges[]` entry (`upstreamKey: /^[a-z0-9][a-z0-9-]{0,62}$/`, `upstreamSlug: /^[A-Z0-9_]{1,64}$/`), and `bin/sdlc-onboard.mjs <owner/repo> [--downstream <owner/repo>] [--instructions "..."]` (plus `.github/workflows/sdlc-onboard.md`) inspects any target repository's `package.json`, workflows, test scripts, and provenance files to synthesize a validated `projects[]` / `dependencyEdges[]` block in `sdlc.yaml` and invoke `S1` (`C1`). Furthermore, `S3`'s router triggers downstream cascades on **any** advance of an upstream repository's `defaultBranch` (whether produced by an `S2` automated drift PR or pushed/merged directly by a human maintainer). Your four upstream repositories (`johnhain-msft/gh-admin-msft`, `ms-mfg-community/day-in-the-life-copilot-lab`, `ms-mfg-community/github-pipeline-showcase`, `githubschool/gh-github-intermediate-template`) and downstream hub (`johnhain-msft/portfolio`) are shipped as the default declarative `sdlc.yaml` fleet configuration and end-to-end integration test fixture.
- **Design Decision — Central Orchestrator Topology & Non-Destructive In-Repo Automation Deconfliction (`CentralRepoOps` + `DeterministicOps`)**: `johnhain-msft/mfg_msft_ai_sdlc` acts as the central automation repository holding schedules, credentials, and compiled `gh-aw` workflows [F552], checking out target upstream and downstream repositories into workspace subdirectories via `checkout:` with `path:` and `github-app:` [F416, F419, F421, F435]. Target repositories require zero installed workflows or repository variables to be audited or cascaded into `portfolio` [F552]. In particular, `S5` **never** sets `GH_AW_POLICY_ALLOW_CREATE_PULL_REQUEST="false"` on `ms-mfg-community/day-in-the-life-copilot-lab` (because `F408` states that variable blocks *every* workflow using `safe-outputs.create-pull-request` in the repo, which would break learner exercises using `.github/workflows/generate-prd.md`). Instead, `sdlc.yaml` provides an per-upstream `deconflictInRepoSchedules: enum("none", "disable-workflow-schedules")` option (default `"none"` so third-party operators bootstrapping their own copy never mutate external upstream repositories): when set to `"disable-workflow-schedules"` by an operator with upstream admin access, `S5` disables only the specific duplicate scheduled workflow IDs (`weekly-content-audit.lock.yml` `cron: "0 5 * * 0"` [F191, F192, F554] and `self-improving-agents.lock.yml` `cron: "0 6 1-7 * 1"` [F222, F244]) via `gh workflow disable` while leaving `generate-prd.lock.yml`, `code-review.lock.yml`, and all `.github/workflows/*.md` and `.lock.yml` files untouched in Git [F139, F244]; and regardless of whether upstream schedules are disabled, `S2` queries open pull requests (`labels: content-audit` / `self-improving-agents` or branch prefixes `automation/*` and `sdlc/*` [F198, F217, F225, F234]) in `pre-agent-steps:` and skips or updates rather than opening a duplicate PR.
- **Design Decision — Single State Owner (`S3`), Concurrency Partitioning, Terminal Cursor States & Provenance-Verified Post-`safe_outputs` Advancement**: `S3` is the **sole owner and writer** of upstream poll/sync state in `johnhain-msft/mfg_msft_ai_sdlc` (`S4` never mutates poll state directly). Poll state is partitioned into **per-upstream Actions variables** `SDLC_SYNC_STATE_<SLUG>` (`SDLC_SYNC_STATE_GH_ADMIN_MSFT`, `SDLC_SYNC_STATE_COPILOT_LAB`, `SDLC_SYNC_STATE_PIPELINE_SHOWCASE`, `SDLC_SYNC_STATE_GITHUB_INTERMEDIATE` [F586, F592, F600, F610]) paired with a canonical lowercase kebab-case `upstreamKey` (`gh-admin-msft`, `copilot-lab`, `pipeline-showcase`, `github-intermediate`) and seeded by `S5` (create-if-missing on `404`) from each edge's `initialBaselineSha` in `sdlc.yaml`. In `cascade-router.yml`, each per-upstream matrix job declares job-level `concurrency: { group: "sdlc-state-${{ matrix.upstreamKey }}", "cancel-in-progress": false }`, while each cascade `gh-aw` workflow declares top-level workflow `concurrency: { group: "sdlc-state-<upstreamKey>", "cancel-in-progress": false }` [F350]; custom post-`safe_outputs` (`if: success()`) and failure-escalation (`if: failure()`) jobs inside a cascade workflow **never** redeclare a job-level `concurrency` group (which would deadlock against their own parent workflow run) and instead execute inside the parent workflow's held `sdlc-state-<upstreamKey>` lock. Each `SDLC_SYNC_STATE_<SLUG>` variable stores `{ etag: string, lastObservedHeadSha: string, lastMergedUpstreamSha: string, portfolioMainSha: nullable<string>, lastNoopHeadSha: nullable<string>, dismissedTargetSha: nullable<string>, failedTargetSha: nullable<string>, failedAttempts: number, blockedTargetSha: nullable<string>, inFlightRunId: nullable<number>, inFlightTargetSha: nullable<string>, openPrTargetSha: nullable<string>, openPrNumber: nullable<number>, updatedAt: string }`. On a conditional-GET `304 Not Modified` [F525], `S3` sets `targetSha = lastObservedHeadSha` and still compares `targetSha` against the terminal states (`lastMergedUpstreamSha`, `lastNoopHeadSha`, `dismissedTargetSha`, `blockedTargetSha`), any active `inFlightTargetSha`, and verified `openPrTargetSha` (checking `GET /repos/johnhain-msft/portfolio/pulls/{openPrNumber}`: if `merged == true`, `lastMergedUpstreamSha` advances to `openPrTargetSha`; if `state == "closed"` and `merged == false`, `dismissedTargetSha = openPrTargetSha` is recorded so a human-rejected PR is not re-opened for the same SHA; when reading `provenanceFile` on `portfolio` `main` returns `404` prior to the first merged cascade PR for `pipeline-showcase` or `github-intermediate`, `S3` falls back to `lastMergedUpstreamSha` seeded from `initialBaselineSha`). If `portfolio` `main`'s `HEAD` SHA has changed since `portfolioMainSha` (`currentPortfolioMainSha != portfolioMainSha`), or if `cascade-router.yml` was triggered via `workflow_dispatch` with boolean `inputs.reset_blocked == true`, or if `targetSha != failedTargetSha`, `S3` resets `failedTargetSha = null`, `failedAttempts = 0`, and `blockedTargetSha = null`, and updates `portfolioMainSha = currentPortfolioMainSha`. When `S3` dispatches `S4` via `workflow_dispatch` (`X-GitHub-Api-Version: 2022-11-28`, `return_run_details: true` [F474, F517]), `S3` records `inFlightRunId = workflow_run_id` and `inFlightTargetSha = targetSha` in `SDLC_SYNC_STATE_<SLUG>` and **exits immediately**, releasing `sdlc-state-<upstreamKey>` so the queued `S4` workflow starts without deadlock. If a cascade workflow fails, its `if: failure()` escalation job sets `failedAttempts = (failedTargetSha == upstream_sha ? failedAttempts + 1 : 1)` and `failedTargetSha = upstream_sha` in `SDLC_SYNC_STATE_<SLUG>`, and once `failedAttempts >= 2` sets `blockedTargetSha = upstream_sha` (failing closed for that `upstream_sha`). For successful `gh-aw` runs (`C8`, `C9`), `S3`/`S4`'s post-`safe_outputs` finalizer job (`needs: [agent, detection, safe_outputs]`, `if: success()` [F364, F371]) invokes `node "$GITHUB_WORKSPACE/src/update-poll-state.mjs"`: if the agent made zero commits and left a clean worktree (`AGENT_HEAD_SHA == BASE_SHA` under `if-no-changes: "ignore"` [F388]), it records `lastNoopHeadSha = upstream_sha`; otherwise it queries `GET /repos/johnhain-msft/portfolio/pulls?state=open&head=johnhain-msft:<branchName>` [F393, F473] (`fallback-as-issue: false` [F389]) and reads the experience's committed JSON provenance file at `ref=${pr.head.sha}` (`public/ghec-admin/source.json#commit` [F76], `experiences/copilot-workshop/source-inputs.json#commit` [F166], `experiences/pipeline-showcase/content/provenance.json#commit`, or `experiences/github-intermediate-labs/provenance.json#commit`) to verify `commit == upstream_sha` before setting `openPrTargetSha = upstream_sha`, `openPrNumber = pr.number`, `failedTargetSha = null`, and `failedAttempts = 0`. When upstream advances (`sha1` -> `sha2`) while PR1 (`sha0..sha1`) is still open and unmerged, `S3` dispatches `S4` with `previous_sha = lastMergedUpstreamSha` (`sha0`), so `S4` computes the full cumulative adaptation `lastMergedUpstreamSha..sha2` against `portfolio` `main` without losing `sha0..sha1` changes.
- **Design Decision — Single Cascade Writer per Portfolio Surface, Read-Only `pre-agent-steps:`, Pre-Commit Bundle Build & Workflow-Scoped Gate Protection (`F364`, `F392`, `F405`, `OQ15`, `OQ37`)**: Every `johnhain-msft/portfolio` experience directory has exactly one cascade workflow and branch owner: `S3` (`cascade-mechanical-ghec-admin.md`, branch `sdlc/cascade-ghec-admin`, `allowed-files: ["public/ghec-admin/**"]` [F405]) owns `public/ghec-admin/**`; `S4` (`cascade-ai-copilot-lab.md`, branch `sdlc/cascade-copilot-lab`) owns `experiences/copilot-workshop/**`, `public/workshop/**`, `apps/lab-studio/**`, `README.md`, `public/copilot-project.html`, and `tests/copilot-workshop.test.ts`; `S4` (`cascade-ai-pipeline-showcase.md`, branch `sdlc/cascade-pipeline-showcase`) is the **single** writer for `experiences/pipeline-showcase/**`, `public/pipeline-showcase/**`, and `tests/pipeline-showcase.test.ts`; and `S4` (`cascade-ai-github-intermediate.md`, branch `sdlc/cascade-github-intermediate`) owns `experiences/github-intermediate-labs/**`, `experiences/github-intermediate-workshop/**`, `public/github-intermediate/**`, and `tests/github-intermediate-{labs,workshop}.test.ts`. `pre-agent-steps:` is strictly read-only on the `portfolio` checkout (recording `BASE_SHA="$(git -C "$GITHUB_WORKSPACE/portfolio" rev-parse HEAD)"` and writing drift reports under `/tmp/gh-aw/agent/`), so if the agent determines zero changes are needed, the worktree stays clean (`AGENT_HEAD_SHA == BASE_SHA`) and the `"noop-no-changes"` path succeeds cleanly. When the agent adapts content (or when `on_commit_only_advance == "bump-provenance"`), before calling `create_pull_request` [F392, `OQ37`] the agent runs the deterministic build/accept/pin helpers (`node "$GITHUB_WORKSPACE/src/bump-studio-revision-pins.mjs" --portfolio "$GITHUB_WORKSPACE/portfolio" --new-sha "$UPSTREAM_SHA"` which reads the prior SHAs from `apps/lab-studio/lab-sources.json#_provenance.commit` [F172] and `experiences/copilot-workshop/provenance.json#commit` [F166] and updates the 40-hex/7-hex revision-pin literals in the target files verified by `SPEC-04` without modifying `//` or `/* */` comments; `npm run sync:workshop-source -- <checkout> --accept`; `node "$GITHUB_WORKSPACE/src/lab-studio-drift.mjs" --accept`; writing `provenance.json` for `pipeline-showcase` or `github-intermediate`; and `npm run build:workshop` / `npm run build:experiences`) and commits all resulting source, test, `public/**`, and provenance files. In `post-steps:`, `node --frozen-intrinsics "$GITHUB_WORKSPACE/src/verify-immutable-gates.mjs" --portfolio "$GITHUB_WORKSPACE/portfolio" --base "$BASE_SHA" --workflow <workflow-id>` (`C7 (2)(c)`, depending on `OQ15`) enforces zero diff against `"$BASE_SHA"` on all always-locked rule/auth/runtime/sandbox files (including `experiences/copilot-workshop/content/rules.ts`, `experiences/copilot-workshop/content/source.ts`, `experiences/copilot-workshop/content/types.ts`, `experiences/copilot-workshop/src/**`, `src/**`, `scripts/**`, `tests/auth.test.ts`, `tests/runtime.test.ts`, `tests/catalog.test.ts`, `tests/copilot-workshop-geometry.test.ts`, `tests/copilot-workshop-runtime.test.ts`, `apps/lab-studio/server/src/{index,copilot-bridge,sandbox,sim-terminal,skills}.ts`, `apps/lab-studio/server/tests/sandbox.test.ts`, and the two sibling experiences' test files — so every `.ts` file in `rules.ts`'s own static import graph `rules.ts -> source.ts` is 100% locked), parses the active workflow's companion test file(s) (matching single- or double-quoted test titles and exiting `1` if any named block is missing in `<base>` or `HEAD`) to verify byte-for-byte preservation of the file prelude, `describe(` headers, and named safety/rule blocks (while rejecting any added `.skip`, `.only`, `.todo`, `vi.mock`, `Object.assign`, `Object.defineProperty`, `Reflect.*`, property mutation, local identifier shadowing, or any new imports of `content/rules` or `tests/support/customer-terms`), executes the invariant checks (`rules.checkCourse(course)`, 140-WPM / audience-word / module-budget invariants, and `findCustomerTerms`) directly in-process under `--frozen-intrinsics` after recursively `Object.freeze`-ing all `content/rules.ts` and `tests/support/customer-terms.ts` exports *before* importing any editable `content/*.ts` module, and runs Vitest with `--reporter=json` to verify that every named locked test title actually executed and reported `status === "passed"`, followed by re-running the build, `git status --porcelain` clean-tree check, and the 5 portfolio verification commands (`typecheck`, `lint`, `test`, `test:studio`, `test:browser` [F10, F11, F15, F16]).
- **Design Decision — Dual Authentication Model & Bot Role Allowlisting**: GitHub API reads, cross-repo checkouts, workflow dispatches, and `safe-outputs` PR creation use a dedicated GitHub App (`vars.APP_CLIENT_ID` and `secrets.APP_PRIVATE_KEY` [F435, F438, F443, F444, F495, F496]) so minted installation access tokens expire after 1 hour [F448, F452, F457] and PRs created by the App automatically trigger target-repository CI checks without awaiting manual run approval [F406, F485-F488, F494, F499]. Because `gh-aw`'s `pre_activation` role check treats a GitHub App sender as never being a repository collaborator and requires it to be allowlisted with `on.bots:` [F363, F370, F543], `S1` renders `on.bots: ["<app-slug>[bot]"]` [F445, F450, F566] into every compiled `gh-aw` workflow's `on:` block. AI engine inference uses either `permissions: copilot-requests: write` (when organization centralized Copilot billing is enabled) or a fine-grained user PAT in `secrets.COPILOT_GITHUB_TOKEN` (since `COPILOT_GITHUB_TOKEN` does not support GitHub Apps or `gho_` OAuth tokens) [F427-F429, F496].
- **Design Decision — Runtime, Toolchain & `gh-aw` Version Isolation**: All Node.js steps in `mfg_msft_ai_sdlc` and target `portfolio` jobs run on Node.js 24 (`ubuntu-24.04` / `ubuntu-latest` [F356]) with Microsoft Edge installed for Playwright `npm run test:browser` checks, satisfying `portfolio`'s `engines.node >=22` and documented Node.js 24 + Edge requirements [F1-F3, F15, F16, F133, F134]. The orchestrator pins `gh-aw` to `>=v1.0.1` (`1.0.1`, released 2026-10-27 with Copilot CLI `1.0.103`), ensuring the fix for `GHSA-8h78-hpm7-29gg` (`>=0.85.4`) and lock-safe PR outputs [F345, F346, F375]; when `S2`'s `post-steps:` verification gate executes `ms-mfg-community/day-in-the-life-copilot-lab`'s own test suite (which checks `gh_aw_schema_version: "0.86.2"` [F241, F253, F381]), it runs the target-repo test subprocess with an isolated `GH_DATA_DIR` containing `gh-aw` `v0.86.2` [F375] so the orchestrator's `>=v1.0.1` runtime and lockfiles are never downgraded.
- **Design Decision — Handling of Open Questions (`§4`)**: No unverified or contradicted item in `§4` (`OQ1`–`OQ51`) is assumed by assertion. Every open question is assigned to exactly one owning child specification in `§6` (`SPEC-01`: `OQ30`–`OQ32`, `OQ38`–`OQ43`; `SPEC-02`: `OQ22`–`OQ25`, `OQ28`–`OQ29`, `OQ34`–`OQ37`; `SPEC-03`: `OQ1`–`OQ3`, `OQ45`–`OQ48`, `OQ51`; `SPEC-04`: `OQ4`–`OQ21`, `OQ26`–`OQ27`; `SPEC-05`: `OQ33`, `OQ44`, `OQ49`–`OQ50`), which must verify the underlying source files before implementation.

| F343 | gh-aw (GitHub Agentic Workflows) lets developers define AI-powered repository automation in Markdown with YAML frontmatter and run AI agents through GitHub Actions. The `gh-aw` GitHub CLI extension compiles each agentic workflow into a standard GitHub Actions workflow. The README says to use conventional GitHub Actions for deterministic builds, tests, linting, deployments and reproducible scripts, and that agentic workflows complement existing CI/CD rather than replace it (README.md lines 16-18). | https://raw.githubusercontent.com/github/gh-aw/b558b97b0a198d3c244154a97a4fcc182ce48126/README.md | 2026-10-26 | VERIFIED |
| F344 | The github/gh-aw repository is under the MIT License with the notice 'Copyright GitHub, Inc.' (LICENSE lines 1-3). | https://raw.githubusercontent.com/github/gh-aw/b558b97b0a198d3c244154a97a4fcc182ce48126/LICENSE | 2026-02-12 | VERIFIED |
| F345 | On the github/gh-aw Releases page, v1.0.1 was published 2026-10-27 and is not flagged as a pre-release. Its notes list: lock-safe pull request outputs (code-producing workflows use lock-safe PR outputs, with a suggested lock exclusion when workflow-permission pushes fail); optional actions-lock integration in `compile`; interactive org and enterprise update rollouts; Copilot CLI upgraded to 1.0.103 with a reviewed checksum pin. | https://github.com/github/gh-aw/releases/tag/v1.0.1 | 2026-10-27 | VERIFIED |
| F346 | Security advisory GHSA-8h78-hpm7-29gg (critical, published 2026-08-27, 'Safe-output artifacts may expose CI trigger tokens'): gh-aw >= 0.83.3 and < 0.85.4 wrote raw process output into the safe-outputs-items artifact. A non-empty `GH_AW_CI_TRIGGER_TOKEN` could therefore be recovered from artifacts of the `create-pull-request` or `push-to-pull-request-branch` safe outputs; staged, cross-repository or skipped paths are not affected. Fixed in 0.85.4. Remediation is to upgrade, run `gh aw upgrade` and commit the regenerated `.lock.yml` files, because 'Upgrading the locally installed CLI without regenerating the workflows does not replace their SHA-pinned action references.' | https://github.com/github/gh-aw/security/advisories/GHSA-8h78-hpm7-29gg | 2026-08-27 | VERIFIED |
| F347 | A gh-aw workflow has two parts: YAML frontmatter wrapped in `---` for configuration and a Markdown body of agent instructions. Agentic workflows live in `.github/workflows` as `*.md` files and compile to GitHub Actions `*.lock.yml` files via `gh aw compile`. The best-practice line says to use descriptive kebab-case names and commit both the `.md` sources and the generated `.lock.yml` files (workflow-structure.md lines 8, 32-43, 71). | https://raw.githubusercontent.com/github/gh-aw/b558b97b0a198d3c244154a97a4fcc182ce48126/docs/src/content/docs/reference/workflow-structure.md | 2026-10-22 | VERIFIED |
| F348 | The Markdown body is loaded at runtime, so body-only edits (instructions, output templates, context, conditions, examples) take effect on the next run without recompilation. Source and lock file should be committed together (working-with-workflows.mdx lines 97-109). | https://raw.githubusercontent.com/github/gh-aw/b558b97b0a198d3c244154a97a4fcc182ce48126/docs/src/content/docs/setup/working-with-workflows.mdx | 2026-10-22 | VERIFIED |

| F353 | `strict:` defaults to true. Workflows compiled with `strict: false` cannot run on public repositories (they fail at runtime asking for recompilation in strict mode). Setting `strict: true` at the top level of `.github/workflows/aw.json` enforces strict mode for every `gh aw compile` in the repository, and that repository setting accepts only `true` (frontmatter.md lines 790-802). | https://raw.githubusercontent.com/github/gh-aw/b558b97b0a198d3c244154a97a4fcc182ce48126/docs/src/content/docs/reference/frontmatter.md | 2026-10-30 | VERIFIED |
| F354 | `gh aw compile --strict` enforces security best practices: no write permissions (use safe-outputs), explicit `network` config, no wildcard domains, pinned actions and no deprecated fields (cli.md line 479). | https://raw.githubusercontent.com/github/gh-aw/b558b97b0a198d3c244154a97a4fcc182ce48126/docs/src/content/docs/setup/cli.md | 2026-11-04 | VERIFIED |
| F355 | Do not use `${{ secrets.* }}` expressions in the workflow-level `env:` section. Those variables are passed directly to the agent container, so secret values would be visible to the AI model. In strict mode this is a compilation error and otherwise a warning; the docs say to use engine-specific secret configuration instead (frontmatter.md lines 475-478). | https://raw.githubusercontent.com/github/gh-aw/b558b97b0a198d3c244154a97a4fcc182ce48126/docs/src/content/docs/reference/frontmatter.md | 2026-10-30 | VERIFIED |
| F356 | Supported `runs-on` runners are `ubuntu-latest` (default), `ubuntu-24.04`/`ubuntu-22.04` and `ubuntu-24.04-arm`. `macos-*` is unsupported because Docker is unavailable on macOS runners, and `windows-*` is unsupported because AWF requires Linux (frontmatter.md lines 450-458). `runs-on-slim` (default `ubuntu-slim`) applies to framework jobs; safe-output jobs that create or push pull requests use `ubuntu-latest` by default because they check out and publish repository code (lines 444-446). | https://raw.githubusercontent.com/github/gh-aw/b558b97b0a198d3c244154a97a4fcc182ce48126/docs/src/content/docs/reference/frontmatter.md | 2026-10-30 | VERIFIED |
| F357 | Defaults: `timeout-minutes` bounds the agentic step and defaults to 20 minutes; the generated agent job defaults to 60 minutes and the detection job to 10. Top-level `max-turns` defaults to 500. `max-ai-credits` is enabled by default at 1000 per run (negative disables it). `max-daily-ai-credits` is disabled unless set (frontmatter.md lines 441-448, 492, 510, 525). | https://raw.githubusercontent.com/github/gh-aw/b558b97b0a198d3c244154a97a4fcc182ce48126/docs/src/content/docs/reference/frontmatter.md | 2026-10-30 | VERIFIED |
| F358 | Frontmatter `source:` tracks workflow origin as `owner/repo/path@ref` and is populated automatically by `gh aw add`. `redirect:` marks a moved workflow (compile skips a file that has `redirect:` but no `on:`). `private: true` blocks installation into other repositories via `gh aw add` (frontmatter.md lines 723-741, 764-774). | https://raw.githubusercontent.com/github/gh-aw/b558b97b0a198d3c244154a97a4fcc182ce48126/docs/src/content/docs/reference/frontmatter.md | 2026-10-30 | VERIFIED |
| F359 | Custom safe-output jobs are defined under `safe-outputs.jobs:`; they run after the agent completes and can expose a custom safe-output tool to the agent. `safe-outputs.report-failed-jobs: false` disables the automatic failed-job reporting issue (frontmatter.md lines 394-418). | https://raw.githubusercontent.com/github/gh-aw/b558b97b0a198d3c244154a97a4fcc182ce48126/docs/src/content/docs/reference/frontmatter.md | 2026-10-30 | VERIFIED |
| F360 | `check-for-updates` (default true) makes the activation job verify that the compiled version is not blocked and meets the minimum supported version. Setting it to false disables the check and is not allowed in strict mode (frontmatter.md lines 776-784). | https://raw.githubusercontent.com/github/gh-aw/b558b97b0a198d3c244154a97a4fcc182ce48126/docs/src/content/docs/reference/frontmatter.md | 2026-10-30 | VERIFIED |
| F361 | Custom MCP servers are declared in a top-level `mcp-servers:` block with options `command`+`args`, `container`, `url`+`headers`, `registry`, `env`, `allowed` and `required` (tools.md lines 280-294). | https://raw.githubusercontent.com/github/gh-aw/b558b97b0a198d3c244154a97a4fcc182ce48126/docs/src/content/docs/reference/tools.md | 2026-10-22 | VERIFIED |
| F362 | Schedules accept fuzzy forms (`daily`, `weekly on monday`, `every 2h`, `daily around 14:00`, `daily between 9:00 and 17:00`). The compiler converts them to cron with a deterministic, file-path-based scattered time and keeps the original text as a comment. Standard cron is also accepted with an optional IANA `timezone`. `every N minutes` has a 5-minute minimum (triggers.md lines 83-124). | https://raw.githubusercontent.com/github/gh-aw/b558b97b0a198d3c244154a97a4fcc182ce48126/docs/src/content/docs/reference/triggers.md | 2026-11-04 | VERIFIED |
| F363 | `on: repository_dispatch` (optional `types:`) lets any system that can POST to /repos/<owner>/<repo>/dispatches (with a `repo`-scoped classic PAT or a token with `contents: write`) start an agentic workflow. `client_payload` fields are usable via `${{ github.event.client_payload.* }}`. Unless `on.roles: all` is set the sender is role-checked: a PAT-based sender is checked against `on.roles`, while 'a GitHub App sender is never a repository collaborator and must be allowlisted with `on.bots:`' (triggers.md lines 362-398). | https://raw.githubusercontent.com/github/gh-aw/b558b97b0a198d3c244154a97a4fcc182ce48126/docs/src/content/docs/reference/triggers.md | 2026-11-04 | VERIFIED |
| F364 | Deterministic hooks around the agent: `pre-steps:` run before checkout, `steps:` after checkout and runtime setup but before the final pre-agent phase, `pre-agent-steps:` just before MCP startup and engine execution, and `post-steps:` after the engine finishes. `jobs:` defines custom jobs that run before the agent, or after it with `needs: [agent, detection]`. The page states that all custom steps and jobs run outside the firewall sandbox with standard GitHub Actions security (steps-jobs.md lines 8, 14-19, 110-114). | https://raw.githubusercontent.com/github/gh-aw/b558b97b0a198d3c244154a97a4fcc182ce48126/docs/src/content/docs/reference/steps-jobs.md | 2026-11-03 | VERIFIED |
| F365 | The DeterministicOps pattern combines deterministic `steps:`/`jobs:` with agent reasoning. Files that custom steps place in `/tmp/gh-aw/agent/` are automatically uploaded as artifacts and available to the AI agent. The flow is deterministic steps -> artifacts -> AI agent -> safe-output jobs (deterministic-ops.md lines 9-27). | https://raw.githubusercontent.com/github/gh-aw/b558b97b0a198d3c244154a97a4fcc182ce48126/docs/src/content/docs/patterns/deterministic-ops.md | 2026-08-26 | VERIFIED |
| F366 | The architecture page states: 'Authored pre-activation, setup/post-steps, custom jobs, detection steps, and safe-output steps/actions are trusted executable code, not automatically confined to the agent sandbox.' Their effective job permissions, secrets, host mounts and network access determine their authority (architecture.mdx line 50). | https://raw.githubusercontent.com/github/gh-aw/b558b97b0a198d3c244154a97a4fcc182ce48126/docs/src/content/docs/introduction/architecture.mdx | 2026-10-28 | VERIFIED |
| F367 | `imports:` composes shared components from other workflow files. Remote imports (for example `acme-org/shared-workflows/shared/security-setup.md@v2.1.0`) are cached under `.github/aw/imports/` by commit SHA after the first fetch. The compiled `.lock.yml` records the exact commit SHA of every remote import, giving reproducible offline compilation (sharing-workflows.md lines 26-34). | https://raw.githubusercontent.com/github/gh-aw/b558b97b0a198d3c244154a97a4fcc182ce48126/docs/src/content/docs/guides/sharing-workflows.md | 2026-10-22 | VERIFIED |
| F368 | `gh aw add` adds the workflow Markdown, generates its lock file, records its `source` for later updates, and fetches declared `resources` and workflows referenced by `dispatch-workflow`. Workflows marked `private: true` cannot be added to another repository (working-with-workflows.mdx line 88). | https://raw.githubusercontent.com/github/gh-aw/b558b97b0a198d3c244154a97a4fcc182ce48126/docs/src/content/docs/setup/working-with-workflows.mdx | 2026-10-22 | VERIFIED |
| F369 | `gh aw compile` validates frontmatter against the workflow schema, validates expression safety (only allow-listed GitHub Actions expressions) and resolves imports with a deterministic breadth-first traversal. It then builds jobs, validates dependencies, pins actions to SHAs and assembles the `.lock.yml` (metadata header, Mermaid dependency graph, alphabetical jobs, embedded original prompt). The Markdown body is loaded at runtime rather than compiled in (compilation-process.md lines 12-14, 54, 64-66, 92-97). | https://raw.githubusercontent.com/github/gh-aw/b558b97b0a198d3c244154a97a4fcc182ce48126/docs/src/content/docs/reference/compilation-process.md | 2026-11-01 | VERIFIED |
| F370 | Compilation generates these jobs: `pre_activation` (role checks, deadlines, skip-if, command triggers); `activation` (prepares context, sanitizes event text, validates lock file freshness); `agent` (runs the engine with tools and MCP); `detection` (scans agent output for threats); safe-output jobs (GitHub API writes); `conclusion` (compilation-process.md lines 133-144). | https://raw.githubusercontent.com/github/gh-aw/b558b97b0a198d3c244154a97a4fcc182ce48126/docs/src/content/docs/reference/compilation-process.md | 2026-11-01 | VERIFIED |
| F371 | Agent, detection, safe_outputs and conclusion are separate jobs because Actions permissions are per-job. The doc's table gives detection `contents: read`; safe_outputs `contents: write`, `issues: write`, `pull-requests: write`; conclusion `issues: write`, `pull-requests: write`, `discussions: write`. The `safe_outputs` job condition `needs.detection.outputs.success == 'true'` prevents it from starting if detection fails (compilation-process.md lines 204-218). | https://raw.githubusercontent.com/github/gh-aw/b558b97b0a198d3c244154a97a4fcc182ce48126/docs/src/content/docs/reference/compilation-process.md | 2026-11-01 | VERIFIED |
| F372 | When recompiling a workflow that already has a lock file, the compiler enforces 'safe update mode': newly added secrets or custom actions not present in the previous manifest require explicit approval via `gh aw compile --approve`. | F373 | `gh aw compile` supports compiling all or named workflows, `--watch`, `--validate --strict`, `--fix`, `--zizmor`/`--poutine`/`--actionlint` scanners, `--no-emit` (validate without writing), `--purge` (remove orphaned `.lock.yml`), `--dry-run`, `--json` (array of ValidationResult) and `--engine`. `gh aw validate` is equivalent to `gh aw compile --validate --no-emit --zizmor --actionlint --poutine` (cli.md lines 418-435, 443, 473, 492). | https://raw.githubusercontent.com/github/gh-aw/b558b97b0a198d3c244154a97a4fcc182ce48126/docs/src/content/docs/setup/cli.md | 2026-11-04 | VERIFIED |
| F374 | The repository-level config schema for `.github/workflows/aw.json` (repo_config_schema.json, `additionalProperties: false`) defines `protected_files.exclude`, `strict` (only true accepted), `ghes`, `action_pins`/`action_pin_prefixes`/`container_pins` (mirrors), `require_self_hosted_runners`, `lock_file_logo`, `admission`, `gateway-dispatch`, `work_queue`, `artifact_retention_days`, `help_command`, `utc`, `auto_upgrade` and `maintenance`. `auto_upgrade` controls generation of `agentic-auto-upgrade.yml` (default false/opt-in; true = default fuzzy weekly schedule; object form takes `cron` and `options: [--pre-releases]` passed to `gh aw upgrade`). `maintenance: false` disables the maintenance workflow and refuses features that rely on it such as `expires` (lines 19-216). | https://raw.githubusercontent.com/github/gh-aw/b558b97b0a198d3c244154a97a4fcc182ce48126/pkg/parser/schemas/repo_config_schema.json | 2026-10-29 | VERIFIED |
| F375 | `gh extension install github/gh-aw` installs the CLI. The docs show pinning with `gh extension install github/gh-aw@v0.1.0` (release tag) or `@<commit sha>`. The standalone installer verifies the binary against the release's `checksums.txt`. | F378 | `gh aw deploy <workflow...> --repo owner/repo` (or `--org` with `--repos`) clones the target repository into an isolated shallow checkout, refreshes existing sourced workflows, adds the requested workflows, recompiles lock files with purge enabled and opens a pull request titled 'chore: deploy agentic workflows'. The default `--cool-down` is `7d` (cli.md lines 1289-1306). | https://raw.githubusercontent.com/github/gh-aw/b558b97b0a198d3c244154a97a4fcc182ce48126/docs/src/content/docs/setup/cli.md | 2026-11-04 | VERIFIED |

| F391 | FAQ: some organizations block PR creation by GitHub Actions (Settings -> Actions -> General -> Workflow permissions), producing 'GitHub Actions is not permitted to create or approve pull requests'. | F392 | How `create-pull-request` applies: the agent's commits are packaged as a git bundle and uploaded as an Actions artifact. A separate `safe_outputs` job then (1) checks out the target repository at the base branch (shallow, depth 1), (2) imports the bundle offline with `git bundle unbundle`, and (3) pushes the branch via the GitHub GraphQL API (signed commits) and creates the PR. On a non-fast-forward race it creates a fallback PR from a temporary branch. An older patch transport (`git format-patch` / `git am --3way`) is used when bundle data is unavailable (safe-outputs-pull-requests.md lines 149-161). | https://raw.githubusercontent.com/github/gh-aw/b558b97b0a198d3c244154a97a4fcc182ce48126/docs/src/content/docs/reference/safe-outputs-pull-requests.md | 2026-11-03 | VERIFIED |

The page also says secondary limits are subject to change without notice. | https://docs.github.com/en/rest/using-the-rest-api/rate-limits-for-the-rest-api | 2026-09-29 | VERIFIED |
| F603 | github/gh-aw (GitHub's agentic-workflows CLI) bootstrap implements the manifest flow with a loopback server (bootstrap_profile_github_app.go lines 185-251): it generates a random hex 'state', opens a TCP listener (netListener uses 127.0.0.1:0), sets the manifest redirect_url to http://<listener-address>/callback, serves a local /register page, opens the browser (or prints the URL) and waits with a timeout for the callback. These files are identical in release v1.0.1 and main. | https://raw.githubusercontent.com/github/gh-aw/ad836943d46bae95b29aa43d537a34719a803beb/pkg/cli/bootstrap_profile_github_app.go | 2026-07-29 | VERIFIED |
| F604 | gh-aw builds its manifest (bootstrap_profile_helpers.go lines 226-255) with name, url, hook_attributes {url: homepage, active: false}, redirect_url, public: false, request_oauth_on_install: false, description, default_permissions (default {metadata: read}) and default_events (default empty list). It posts it to https://github.com/organizations/<owner>/settings/apps/new?state=<state> for organization owners, otherwise https://github.com/settings/apps/new?state=<state>. App names are sanitized to at most 34 characters (lines 196-224). | https://raw.githubusercontent.com/github/gh-aw/01e43c979ab873a8ad2f948d11e06ee1a74e752c/pkg/cli/bootstrap_profile_helpers.go | 2026-10-04 | VERIFIED |
| F605 | gh-aw's local registration page is a static HTML form with method='post' to the registration URL, containing a hidden input named 'manifest', auto-submitted by script (with a noscript fallback button). This is how a CLI hands the manifest POST to the browser. | https://raw.githubusercontent.com/github/gh-aw/7f798e9238054b7057786caf5c7d6a647e8a22ee/pkg/cli/bootstrap_registration_page.html | 2026-10-02 | VERIFIED |
| F606 | gh-aw's /callback handler (bootstrap_profile_github_app.go lines 286-397) returns HTTP 400 if 'code' is missing, contains characters other than [A-Za-z0-9_-], or 'state' does not match. It then exchanges the code by running gh api -X POST -H 'Accept: application/vnd.github+json' /app-manifests/<code>/conversions, parses html_url, client_id, id, pem, slug and name from the response, and redirects the browser to the app's install URL (https://github.com/apps/<slug>/installations/new). | https://raw.githubusercontent.com/github/gh-aw/ad836943d46bae95b29aa43d537a34719a803beb/pkg/cli/bootstrap_profile_github_app.go | 2026-07-29 | VERIFIED |
| F607 | After the manifest exchange, gh-aw writes the App's client ID to a repository Actions variable (name from the 'app-id-variable' setting) and the PEM private key to a repository Actions secret (name from 'private-key-secret'), then waits for installation (bootstrap_profile_github_app.go lines 68-85). It polls gh api /user/installations?per_page=100 --paginate and, for 'selected' installations, /user/installations/{id}/repositories, matching by client ID, app slug or app ID (lines 399-527). | https://raw.githubusercontent.com/github/gh-aw/ad836943d46bae95b29aa43d537a34719a803beb/pkg/cli/bootstrap_profile_github_app.go | 2026-07-29 | VERIFIED |
| F608 | gh-aw's bootstrap is idempotent and non-interactive-aware. It skips App setup if both the configured repository variable and secret already exist (bootstrap_profile_github_app.go lines 24-30). When not interactive it refuses to create an App ('creating a new GitHub App requires an interactive browser flow') unless existing credentials are supplied via env vars or mode=create is forced (lines 65-67). It discovers existing names via gh api /repos/{repo}/actions/variables?per_page=100 --paginate and /actions/secrets?per_page=100 --paginate (bootstrap_profile_actions_repo.go lines 92-106). | https://raw.githubusercontent.com/github/gh-aw/ad836943d46bae95b29aa43d537a34719a803beb/pkg/cli/bootstrap_profile_github_app.go | 2026-07-29 | VERIFIED |
| F609 | gh-aw bootstrap_profile_runner.go lines 14-25 define a 10-minute timeout for the browser manifest flow, a 5-second installation poll delay, and env-var overrides: GH_AW_BOOTSTRAP_GITHUB_APP_MODE, _OWNER, _NAME, _URL, _DESCRIPTION, _CLIENT_ID, _PRIVATE_KEY and GH_AW_BOOTSTRAP_NO_OPEN_BROWSER. | https://raw.githubusercontent.com/github/gh-aw/30b411e4c7c5fada65e6ca8a847408fd250d63c3/pkg/cli/bootstrap_profile_runner.go | 2026-09-05 | VERIFIED |
| F610 | gh-aw upserts Actions variables by running gh api -X PATCH <variables endpoint>/<name> -f name=... -f value=... first. If that fails with HTTP 404 it runs gh api -X POST <variables endpoint> -f name=... -f value=..., adding -f visibility=... for organization and enterprise scope. An HTTP 422 'missing visibility' error is rewritten to say visibility (all, private, selected) is required (env_command.go lines 635-667; this main-branch file differs from v1.0.1). | https://raw.githubusercontent.com/github/gh-aw/8393d3f1eb38bbb53bdec95f3657c5b655b10cea/pkg/cli/env_command.go | 2026-11-03 | VERIFIED |
| F611 | gh-aw sets a repository secret through the REST API (secret_set_command.go lines 218-288): it GETs repos/{owner}/{repo}/actions/secrets/public-key, requires key_id and key, checks the key is 32 bytes, encrypts with NaCl box.SealAnonymous, Base64-encodes, and PUTs {encrypted_value, key_id} to repos/{owner}/{repo}/actions/secrets/{name}. | https://raw.githubusercontent.com/github/gh-aw/9f9df7b495928ca8ce89dcb0af68a51579f30665/pkg/cli/secret_set_command.go | 2026-10-31 | VERIFIED |
| F612 | On gh-aw main (not in release v1.0.1), actions_storage.go lines 12-21 refuse to store any value containing 'gho_' (the gh auth login OAuth token) as an Actions secret or variable, with the message 'never use the gh auth login OAuth token as a workflow credential'. | https://raw.githubusercontent.com/github/gh-aw/5ec4363a9a818f2dfbc5cd7bd8948bfec7e32e1e/pkg/cli/actions_storage.go | 2026-11-03 | VERIFIED |
| F613 | gh-aw's package-manifest JSON Schema defines a declarative 'github-app' config action ('configure GitHub App credentials') requiring type, app-id-variable and private-key-secret. Optional properties are owner (const 'repo'), name, app-name, homepage-url, mode (create-or-existing \| existing), existing-only, permissions (string map) and events (string array). The same schema defines repo-secret, repo-label, copilot-auth, commit-and-push and handoff actions. | https://raw.githubusercontent.com/github/gh-aw/791ec67b741b33fb82c33db6a966d8a630298781/pkg/parser/schemas/aw_manifest_schema.json | 2026-09-13 | VERIFIED |

## 4. Open Questions

Every claim below did not verify during source adjudication (`CONTRADICTED` or `UNVERIFIED`) and remains open. None of these items is assumed or resolved by assertion in this plan; where a cross-stream contract (`§5`) or child specification (`§6`) touches an open item, it names the `OQ<n>` identifier and requires child-specification verification before implementation.

| ID | Grade | Unverified / Contradicted Claim | Why It Is Open (Verifier Note) |
|---|---|---|---|
| OQ1 | CONTRADICTED | `clean(html, file)` runs sanitize-html with allowed tags = the sanitize-html defaults plus `input`, `label`, `details`, `summary`, `kbd`, `img`; allowed attributes `*`: id, class; `a`: href, title; `img`: src, alt, title; `input`: type, data-check, checked; `code`: class; `th`/`td`: colspan, rowspan; allowed schemes https, http, mailto; and a `transformTags.a` hook that rewrites every `href` through `rewriteLink(attributes.href, file)` (lines 84-94). | The configuration matches, but line 92 rewrites only truthy attributes.href values. An empty href is preserved without calling rewriteLink; an isolated evaluation confirmed this. |
| OQ2 | CONTRADICTED | Markdown documents are parsed with `new MarkdownIt({ html: true, linkify: false })` (line 95); each heading token gets `id = slugger.slug(heading)` from a per-document `GithubSlugger`, the document title is the first heading's text with the file path as fallback (`title: title ?? file`), and the rendered HTML is passed through `clean()` (lines 110-120, 136, 138). | Line 120 uses if (!title), not a first-heading-only assignment. An empty first heading can therefore be replaced by a later heading; title ?? file also preserves an empty title. The parser, slugger and clean calls otherwise match. |

The remaining guard description matches. |

For a root-level pattern such as `*.md`, dirname(path) is `.`, and path.slice(dir.length + 1) discards `*.` before constructing the regex, producing `^md$`. An isolated evaluation of the opened function returned no matches despite a directory listing containing matching Markdown filenames. Directory-prefixed globs follow the stated scheme. |

## 5. Cross-Stream Interface Contracts

| Contract ID | From | To | Request / response payload | State owner | Error / escalation handoff |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| C1 | S5 | S1 | **Request (`BootstrapReconcileRequest`)**: `{ manifestPath: string, appSlug: string, appClientIdVar: string, appPrivateKeySecret: string, pollStateVarPrefix: "SDLC_SYNC_STATE_", engineAuthMode: enum("copilot-requests", "copilot-pat", "api-key"), ghAwVersionPin: string, securityBaseline: { minGhAwVersion: "1.0.1", grantedAppPermissions: Record<string, enum("read", "write")>, requireStrictMode: true, requireThreatDetection: true, forbidAutoMerge: true }, dryRun: boolean }` invoked by `bin/sdlc-bootstrap.mjs` (`S5`) after verifying `gh-aw >= v1.0.1` (`GHSA-8h78-hpm7-29gg` [F345, F346, F375]), completing App registration (`slug` from `POST /app-manifests/{code}/conversions` [F566, F606] or `GET /app` [F578]), and provisioning secrets/variables [F428-F437, F443, F581-F612]. **Response (`ReconcileResult`)**: `{ status: enum("ok", "error"), generatedWorkflows: Array<{ sourceMd: string, lockYml: string }>, repoConfigPath: string, validationResults: Array<{ file: string, valid: boolean, errors: string[] }> }` emitted by `src/reconcile.mjs` (`S1`) after validating `sdlc.yaml` against `securityBaseline` and running `gh aw compile --strict --purge --json` [F353, F354, F369, F373]. Depends on `OQ30`–`OQ32` and `OQ38`–`OQ39` (`S1`) and `OQ33` (`S5`). | `S1` owns `sdlc.yaml`, `schemas/sdlc.schema.json`, `.github/workflows/aw.json` (`strict: true`, `protected_files.exclude` [F353, F374, F405]), and all rendered `.github/workflows/*.md` + `.lock.yml` security and concurrency policies (`concurrency`, `permissions`, `threat-detection`, `protected-files`, `allowed-files`); `S5` owns `aw.yml` and GitHub-side App/variable/secret state. | If `sdlc.yaml` fails JSON Schema validation, requests permissions exceeding `securityBaseline.grantedAppPermissions` [F447, F453, F469], enables `auto-merge` [F236, F407], or `gh aw compile --strict` returns any `ValidationResult` error [F373], `S1` aborts file emission (rolling back uncommitted workflow edits), returns `status: "error"` with compiler diagnostics to `S5`, and `S5` exits non-zero with remediation instructions for `external:operator`. |
| C2 | S1 | S2 | **Request (`UpstreamDriftWorkflowSpec`)**: Reconciled `gh-aw` workflow definitions `.github/workflows/drift-<projectKey>.md` (and `.lock.yml`, including `.github/workflows/drift-gh-admin-msft.md`, `.github/workflows/drift-copilot-lab.md`, and `.github/workflows/drift-self-improving-agents.md` in the default preset) generated by `src/reconcile.mjs` (`S1`) from each project's `driftAudit` configuration `{ enabled: boolean, schedule: string, upkeepStrategy: enum("content-and-docs-sync", "dependency-and-pattern-modernization", "code-quality-and-improvement-scan", "custom"), instructions: string, instructionsFile: nullable<string>, knowledgeSources: { watchUrls: Array<{ url: string, label: string, kind: enum("changelog", "blog", "docs", "release-notes", "feed") }>, packageManifests: string[], mcpServers: object }, buildCommands: string[], verifyCommands: string[], draft: boolean, reviewers: string[] }` in `sdlc.yaml`, rendering typed `gh-aw` frontmatter `{ on: { schedule: [{ cron: string }], workflow_dispatch: { inputs: { target_repo: string, correlation_id: string, extra_instructions: string, dry_run: boolean } }, bots: [string] }, concurrency: { group: string, "cancel-in-progress": false }, permissions: { contents: "read", issues: "read", "pull-requests": "read" }, network: { allowed: string[] }, checkout: [{ repository: string, path: string, current: true, "github-app": { "client-id": string, "private-key": string } }], "mcp-servers": object, "pre-agent-steps": array, "post-steps": array, "safe-outputs": { "github-app": object, "threat-detection": { "continue-on-error": false }, "create-pull-request": { "target-repo": string, "title-prefix": string, labels: string[], reviewers: string[], draft: boolean, "branch-prefix": "sdlc/", "preserve-branch-name": true, "recreate-ref": true, "close-older-pull-requests": true, "close-older-key": string, "exclude-lock-yml": true, "protected-files": { policy: "blocked", exclude: string[] }, "auto-merge": false, "fallback-as-issue": true, max: 1 } } }` where `S1` automatically extracts the exact literal hostnames from `knowledgeSources.watchUrls` into `network.allowed` (satisfying `gh aw compile --strict`'s no-wildcard explicit network rule [F350, F354]) and compiles the user's `instructions` / `instructionsFile`, `upkeepStrategy`, `watchUrls`, and `packageManifests` into the workflow's Markdown prompt body and `pre-agent-steps:` snapshot hooks [F192-F200, F222-F227, F236, F349, F350, F354, F363-F365, F386, F388, F393-F395, F403-F405, F407, F416, F421, F435, F543, F544]. **Response (`DriftWorkflowCompileAck`)**: `{ workflowId: string, lockPath: string, targetRepo: string, allowedNetworkDomains: string[], gateCommands: string[] }`. Depends on `OQ30`–`OQ32` and `OQ38`–`OQ43` (`S1`) and `OQ34`–`OQ37` (`S2`). | `S1` owns the compiled workflow definitions, `network.allowed` domain extraction, `on.bots` allowlist, `concurrency` configuration, and static `safe-outputs` policy; `S2` owns runtime drift/modernization evaluation and generated artifacts under `/tmp/gh-aw/agent/` [F365]. | If a `driftAudit` entry in `sdlc.yaml` has an invalid/wildcard URL in `watchUrls` [F354], omits `instructions` and `instructionsFile`, or is missing runnable `verifyCommands`, `close-older-key`, or `target-repo` checkout mapping [F416], `S1` rejects compilation during reconciliation; at runtime, if `pre_activation`, checkout, or `detection` (`continue-on-error: false` [F386]) fails in `S2`, `safe_outputs` is skipped and the failure is reported in `johnhain-msft/mfg_msft_ai_sdlc` [F359, F370, F371]. |
| C3 | S1 | S3 | **Request (`CascadePipelineSpec`)**: Reconciled event-router/poller workflow `.github/workflows/cascade-router.yml` (with `workflow_dispatch` input `reset_blocked: boolean` and per-upstream matrix jobs serialized under job-level `concurrency: { group: "sdlc-state-${{ matrix.upstreamKey }}", "cancel-in-progress": false }` [F350], where `matrix.upstreamKey` is populated from `sdlc.yaml` `dependencyEdges[]`, e.g., `["gh-admin-msft", "copilot-lab", "pipeline-showcase", "github-intermediate"]` in the default preset) and mechanical cascade `gh-aw` workflows (`.github/workflows/cascade-mechanical-ghec-admin.md` + `.lock.yml` in the default preset, workflow-level `concurrency: { group: "sdlc-state-<upstreamKey>", "cancel-in-progress": false }` [F350]) with generic dependency edges `{ upstreamRepo: string, upstreamKey: string, upstreamSlug: string, upstreamDefaultBranch: string, initialBaselineSha: string, downstreamRepo: string, mode: enum("mechanical", "ai-adaptation"), schedule: nullable<string>, onCommitOnlyAdvance: enum("noop", "bump-provenance"), stateVariable: string, provenanceFile: string, provenanceJsonPointer: string, onBots: [string], branchName: string, closeOlderKey: string, reviewers: string[], importCommands: string[], buildCommands: string[], verifyCommands: string[] }`, `safe-outputs.create-pull-request` configured with `{ "target-repo": string, "title-prefix": "chore(cascade): ", labels: ["automated", "sdlc-cascade", "needs-review"], reviewers: string[], draft: false, "preserve-branch-name": true, "recreate-ref": true, "close-older-pull-requests": true, "close-older-key": string, "if-no-changes": "ignore", "auto-merge": false, "fallback-as-issue": false, "allowed-files": string[], max: 1 }`, and custom post-`safe_outputs` (`if: success()`) and failure-escalation (`if: failure()`) jobs in `jobs:` (`needs: [agent, detection, safe_outputs]`, with **no** job-level `concurrency` field so they run without deadlock inside the parent workflow's `sdlc-state-<upstreamKey>` lock [F350, F364, F371]) that invoke `S3`'s `node "$GITHUB_WORKSPACE/src/update-poll-state.mjs"` (on success verifying via `GET /repos/{downstreamRepo}/pulls?state=open&head={owner}:<branchName>` [F393, F473] and `<provenanceFile>#commit` at `ref=${pr.head.sha}` [F76] that the open PR exists for `upstream_sha`; on failure invoking `--record-failure` to update `failedTargetSha`, `failedAttempts`, and `blockedTargetSha`) [F8-F11, F13, F15, F30, F76, F166, F172, F350, F363, F364, F371, F388, F389, F393, F395, F405, F407, F543, F586, F592]. **Response (`CascadeCompileAck`)**: `{ routerWorkflowPath: string, mechanicalWorkflowPaths: string[], dependencyEdgesCount: number }`. Depends on `OQ38`–`OQ43` (`S1`). | `S1` owns the dependency graph and compiled workflow files; `S3` is the **sole owner and writer** of the durable per-upstream `SDLC_SYNC_STATE_<SLUG>` variables in `johnhain-msft/mfg_msft_ai_sdlc` and mechanical cascade execution state. | If `sdlc.yaml` contains a cycle in `dependencyEdges`, omits `initialBaselineSha`, assigns the same `branchName`, `closeOlderKey`, `upstreamKey`, or `stateVariable` to two edges, sets a job-level `concurrency` duplicating the workflow-level group, omits `reviewers` or `allowed-files` for a downstream target, or references an unknown sync mode, `S1` fails schema/graph validation before emitting workflows; if runtime routing in `S3` fails, `S3` fails the router job without advancing `SDLC_SYNC_STATE_<SLUG>` and opens/updates a `[sdlc-failure]` diagnostic issue in `johnhain-msft/mfg_msft_ai_sdlc`. |
